# 拾頁 StorySteps｜第3級 B本｜全新第七版

# 《把星空借給每一個人》
*The Sky We Carry Together*

**代碼：** 3B　**級距：** 2000–3000 詞　**題材：** 移動星空展、社區故事巡演、女性自主與成年慢熱戀愛。

**主角：** 芮安（28歲，巡迴星空展策展人）、喬森（31歲，展覽機械師）。

## 故事簡介

二十八歲的芮安帶著一座可以折疊的星空帳篷巡迴各個小鎮，想把天文、居民故事與免費公共教育連在一起。三十一歲的機械師喬森負責讓設備安全運作。當贊助商要求以免費入場交換觀眾個資和內容控制權，巡演從一場漂亮的展覽變成一次次必須公開負責的選擇。芮安逐漸明白：她不只要守住自己的創作理想，也要讓其他人保有拒絕、修正與共同決定的權利。

**閱讀說明：** 每一段 EN 英文正文後立即接完整繁體中文對照。每章設有故事中真實使用的「居民詞語圈」，用來練習20個指定詞條（第25章21個）；卡片與對話均置於 EN 正文，並非只出現在章末詞表。詞彙自然度與情節文學品質仍需編輯複核。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜The Fairground Under One Sky｜市集上的第一片星空
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the riverside weekend fair, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: a sponsor had demanded identity scans before anyone could enter the free show. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在河畔週末市集，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：贊助商要求每位觀眾先掃描身分資料才能進入免費展覽。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "berry", "circus", "deed", and "fairy". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A listener remembered berry from an ordinary day. One card about circus brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed deed through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about fairy in a story about the town. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著berry（莓果）、circus（馬戲團）、deed（產權契據）、fairy（仙子）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「莓果」。一張關於「馬戲團」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「產權契據」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「仙子」放入小鎮故事。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "fancy", "heater", "hut", and "jazz". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really fancy and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed heater through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about hut in a story about the town. A listener remembered jazz from an ordinary day. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是fancy（華麗的）、heater（暖爐）、hut（小木屋）、jazz（爵士樂）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工用「華麗的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「暖爐」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「小木屋」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「爵士樂」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: a loose screw had stopped the rotating sky lamp. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui set the lamp by hand and opened the dome without collecting a single name. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當一顆鬆動的螺絲讓旋轉星空燈停止運作，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安用手調整燈具，沒有收集任何姓名就開放星空帳篷。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "jet", "magician", "miracle", and "pint". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed jet through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about magician in a story about the town. A listener remembered miracle from an ordinary day. One card about pint brought two different memories together. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：jet（噴射機）、magician（魔術師）、miracle（奇蹟）、pint（品脫）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「噴射機」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「魔術師」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「奇蹟」。一張關於「品脫」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "political", "quit", "rob", and "screw". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. One elder called the funding decision political because it affected public resources. Someone asked when it would be right to quit. A volunteer used rob in an example about responsibility. Jonas kept a spare screw in the tool box near the dome. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著political（政治的）、quit（辭去、停止）、rob（剝奪、搶走）、screw（螺絲）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有位長者說資助決定具有政治性，因為牽涉公共資源。有人思考什麼時候該「辭去、停止」，又該何時停下。一名志工用「剝奪、搶走」舉出責任的例子。喬森在星空帳篷旁的工具箱放了一顆備用螺絲。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that the printed contract still contained a paragraph about visitor records. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: she marked that paragraph for public discussion before signing anything. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：印好的合約仍有一段涉及觀眾紀錄的條款。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：她把那段條款標記出來，決定在簽署任何文件前公開討論。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "signal", "spin", "tame", and "various". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered signal from an ordinary day. One card about spin brought two different memories together. A visitor said tame did not mean the same thing to every family. Various residents wanted different kinds of stories under the stars. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著signal（訊號）、spin（旋轉）、tame（馴服）、various（各式各樣的）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「訊號」。一張關於「旋轉」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「馴服」對每個家庭不一定相同。各式各樣的居民希望在星空下聽到不同類型的故事。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the riverside weekend fair?**
A. A sponsor had demanded identity scans before anyone could enter the free show.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：贊助商要求每位觀眾先掃描身分資料才能進入免費展覽。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Rui set the lamp by hand and opened the dome without collecting a single name.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安用手調整燈具，沒有收集任何姓名就開放星空帳篷。

## CHAPTER 02｜A Vote About the Route｜誰決定巡演路線
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached the adult college meeting room, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that the first proposed route passed only through rich neighborhoods. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達成人學院的會議室時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：原定巡演路線只經過富裕的社區。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "beast", "bowling", "budget", and "decade". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. One card about beast brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed bowling through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about budget in a story about the town. A listener remembered decade from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是beast（野獸）、bowling（保齡球）、budget（預算）、decade（十年）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一張關於「野獸」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「保齡球」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「預算」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「十年」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "drunk", "expressive", "frighten", and "geography". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. An adult remembered a drunk driver and asked for a safe ride home. A speaker asked whether the word expressive described their memory fairly. Someone asked when it would be right to frighten. One card about geography brought two different memories together. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：drunk（喝醉的）、expressive（善於表達的）、frighten（使害怕）、geography（地理位置）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名成年人想起酒駕者，提醒大家安排安全返家方式。有人稱某段回憶是「善於表達的」，並說明原因。有人思考什麼時候該「使害怕」，又該何時停下。一張關於「地理位置」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: a ten-year-old map ignored villages beyond the hills. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui asked residents to vote on locations after examining travel costs. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：一張十年前的地圖漏掉山丘另一側的村落。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安請居民先檢查交通成本再投票決定站點。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "greedy", "kit", "lace", and "necktie". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A speaker asked whether the word greedy described their memory fairly. A listener remembered kit from an ordinary day. One card about lace brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed necktie through a concrete detail, not a guess. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著greedy（貪婪的）、kit（工具組）、lace（蕾絲）、necktie（領帶）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有人稱某段回憶是「貪婪的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「工具組」。一張關於「蕾絲」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「領帶」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "passenger", "permit", "professor", and "regret". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered passenger from an ordinary day. One card about permit brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed professor through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about regret in a story about the town. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著passenger（乘客）、permit（允許）、professor（教授）、regret（後悔）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「乘客」。一張關於「允許」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「教授」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「後悔」放入小鎮故事。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Success was still uncertain. The sharpest objection was that the sponsor offered money if its favored town stayed first. Rui did not dismiss the worry merely because it came from someone powerful. She separated its real cost from the demand hidden inside it. Then she kept the vote public and refused the private condition. Jonas helped with tools and timings, but he left the final public explanation to the person who had promised the audience a fair program. Later, when they folded the chairs together, he admitted that he had once mistaken agreement for affection. Rui told him she preferred a partner who could argue carefully and still listen. Neither offered a grand promise. The next stop would test them both.

**繁中**
事情是否成功仍不確定。最尖銳的反對聲是：贊助商表示只要把它指定的小鎮排第一就會出資。芮安沒有因為這話來自權勢者就直接否定，而是把真正的成本與藏在其中的要求分開。然後，她維持公開投票並拒絕私下條件。喬森協助工具與時程，卻把公開說明留給曾答應觀眾公平待遇的芮安。稍後兩人一起收椅子時，他承認自己過去曾把意見一致誤當成好感。芮安告訴他，她更喜歡能好好爭論、也願意聽的人。兩人都沒許下宏大的誓言；下一站還會考驗他們。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "risk", "semester", "visible", and "voter". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about risk brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed semester through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word visible described their memory fairly. A listener remembered voter from an ordinary day. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著risk（風險）、semester（學期）、visible（看得見的）、voter（投票者）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「風險」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「學期」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「看得見的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「投票者」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the adult college meeting room?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. The first proposed route passed only through rich neighborhoods.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：原定巡演路線只經過富裕的社區。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. Rui asked residents to vote on locations after examining travel costs.
B. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安請居民先檢查交通成本再投票決定站點。

## CHAPTER 03｜The Map Made of Voices｜用聲音畫出的地圖
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the downtown public library while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until several families could not reach the proposed show by bus. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在市中心的公共圖書館時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：好幾個家庭無法搭公車抵達原定展場。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "background", "bamboo", "bundle", and "chat". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed background through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about bamboo in a story about the town. A listener remembered bundle from an ordinary day. One card about chat brought two different memories together. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：background（背景聲）、bamboo（竹子）、bundle（一束）、chat（聊天）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「背景聲」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「竹子」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「一束」。一張關於「聊天」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "chip", "civil", "dealer", and "deepen". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A neighbor talked about chip in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly civil. One card about dealer brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to deepen in a fair and careful way. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著chip（洋芋片）、civil（民事的）、dealer（商販／經銷商）、deepen（加深）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名鄰居把「洋芋片」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「民事的」。一張關於「商販／經銷商」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「加深」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: a hand-drawn map showed a level path beside a bamboo garden. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Rui walked the route with a wheelchair user and changed the meeting point. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：一張手繪地圖顯示竹園旁有條平緩的路。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，芮安陪輪椅使用者實走那條路並更換集合地點。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "democratic", "dine", "doughnut", and "downtown". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly democratic. A volunteer used dine in an example about responsibility. A volunteer discussed doughnut through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word downtown described their memory fairly. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著democratic（民主的）、dine（用餐）、doughnut（甜甜圈）、downtown（市中心）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「民主的」。一名志工用「用餐」舉出責任的例子。志工把「甜甜圈」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「市中心」，並說明原因。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "leap", "mighty", "pigeon", and "poll". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about leap brought two different memories together. A visitor said mighty did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about pigeon in a story about the town. A listener remembered poll from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著leap（跳躍）、mighty（強大的）、pigeon（鴿子）、poll（民意調查）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「跳躍」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「強大的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「鴿子」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「民意調查」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: a city official called the change too small to matter. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, she recorded the exact distance and asked the official to walk it too. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：一名市府人員說這個改動小到不值得在意。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：她記錄確切距離，並邀請那名官員親自走一次。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "poverty", "react", "sake", and "substance". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed poverty through a concrete detail, not a guess. A listener asked what it meant to react in an everyday choice. A listener remembered sake from an ordinary day. One card about substance brought two different memories together. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是poverty（貧窮）、react（回應）、sake（為了）、substance（實質內容）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「貧窮」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「回應」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「為了」。一張關於「實質內容」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

## CHAPTER 04｜The Red Cable｜紅色纜線
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the old hilltop lookout, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: the proposed entrance crossed a dark service alley. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在山頂的舊瞭望台，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：預定入口必須穿過一條漆黑的維修巷。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "alley", "bravery", "cable", and "cherry". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A neighbor talked about alley in a story about the town. A listener remembered bravery from an ordinary day. One card about cable brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed cherry through a concrete detail, not a guess. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著alley（巷弄）、bravery（勇氣）、cable（電纜）、cherry（櫻桃）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名鄰居把「巷弄」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「勇氣」。一張關於「電纜」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「櫻桃」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "crash", "definition", "dizzy", and "investigate". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered crash from an ordinary day. One card about definition brought two different memories together. A visitor said dizzy did not mean the same thing to every family. A listener asked what it meant to investigate in an everyday choice. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著crash（撞擊）、definition（定義）、dizzy（頭暈的）、investigate（調查）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「撞擊」。一張關於「定義」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「頭暈的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「調查」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: a red cable hidden beneath the platform had worn through its cover. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Jonas isolated the power while Rui moved the audience toward the open square. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當平台下方的一條紅色纜線外皮已經磨損，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，喬森先切斷電源，芮安則把觀眾引導至開放廣場。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "knit", "label", "murder", and "nap". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about knit brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed label through a concrete detail, not a guess. A card mentioned murder in a fictional mystery, not in a claim about a real neighbor. A listener remembered nap from an ordinary day. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著knit（編織）、label（標籤）、murder（謀殺）、nap（打盹、小睡）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「編織」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「標籤」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有張卡在虛構推理故事裡提到謀殺，並非指控真實的鄰居。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「打盹、小睡」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "saucer", "sexy", "solid", and "starve". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed saucer through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word sexy described their memory fairly. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly solid. A volunteer used starve in an example about responsibility. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是saucer（茶碟）、sexy（性感的）、solid（堅固的）、starve（使缺乏）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「茶碟」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「性感的」，並說明原因。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「堅固的」。一名志工用「使缺乏」舉出責任的例子。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that the landowner wanted the opening to go ahead for photographs. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: they postponed the show and published a simple safety notice. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：地主希望為了拍照讓開幕照常進行。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：兩人延後展覽，並公開一份簡明安全公告。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "thankful", "tower", "van", and "warmth". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A speaker asked whether the word thankful described their memory fairly. A listener remembered tower from an ordinary day. One card about van brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed warmth through a concrete detail, not a guess. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：thankful（感謝的）、tower（塔）、van（貨車）、warmth（溫暖）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。有人稱某段回憶是「感謝的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「塔」。一張關於「貨車」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「溫暖」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the old hilltop lookout?**
A. The proposed entrance crossed a dark service alley.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：預定入口必須穿過一條漆黑的維修巷。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Jonas isolated the power while Rui moved the audience toward the open square.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：喬森先切斷電源，芮安則把觀眾引導至開放廣場。

## CHAPTER 05｜The Voice in the Garage｜車庫裡的聲音
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached a converted repair garage, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that a promotional recording used a grandmother's voice without permission. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達改裝的修理車庫時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：一段宣傳錄音未經同意使用了祖母的聲音。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "bacon", "carpet", "complain", and "considerable". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered bacon from an ordinary day. One card about carpet brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to complain in a fair and careful way. A speaker asked whether the word considerable described their memory fairly. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著bacon（培根）、carpet（地毯）、complain（抱怨）、considerable（相當多的）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「培根」。一張關於「地毯」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「抱怨」。有人稱某段回憶是「相當多的」，並說明原因。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "cough", "engage", "fake", and "flashlight". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about cough brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed engage through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word fake described their memory fairly. A listener remembered flashlight from an ordinary day. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著cough（咳嗽）、engage（參與、從事）、fake（假的）、flashlight（手電筒）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「咳嗽」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「參與、從事」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「假的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「手電筒」。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: the original microphone file contained an earlier request to remove the clip. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui replaced the recording with live narration written by volunteers. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：原始麥克風檔案裡保留著要求刪除片段的較早訊息。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安改用志工共同撰寫的現場口述。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "garage", "jaw", "microphone", and "motor". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed garage through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about jaw in a story about the town. A listener remembered microphone from an ordinary day. One card about motor brought two different memories together. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是garage（車庫）、jaw（下巴）、microphone（麥克風）、motor（馬達）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「車庫」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「下巴」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「麥克風」。一張關於「馬達」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "peanut", "pronounce", "responsibility", and "ripe". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A neighbor talked about peanut in a story about the town. A volunteer asked everyone to pronounce a town name as its residents did. One card about responsibility brought two different memories together. A visitor said ripe did not mean the same thing to every family. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：peanut（花生）、pronounce（發音）、responsibility（責任）、ripe（成熟的）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名鄰居把「花生」放入小鎮故事。一名志工請大家按照居民的讀法唸出鎮名。一張關於「責任」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「成熟的」對每個家庭不一定相同。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Success was still uncertain. The sharpest objection was that the producer called the correction a waste of an expensive campaign. Rui did not dismiss the worry merely because it came from someone powerful. She separated its real cost from the demand hidden inside it. Then she returned the clip and established a clear withdrawal form. Jonas helped with tools and timings, but he left the final public explanation to the person who had promised the audience a fair program. Later, when they folded the chairs together, he admitted that he had once mistaken agreement for affection. Rui told him she preferred a partner who could argue carefully and still listen. Neither offered a grand promise. The next stop would test them both.

**繁中**
事情是否成功仍不確定。最尖銳的反對聲是：製作人說這項更正浪費了昂貴的宣傳活動。芮安沒有因為這話來自權勢者就直接否定，而是把真正的成本與藏在其中的要求分開。然後，她歸還錄音片段，並建立清楚的撤回表單。喬森協助工具與時程，卻把公開說明留給曾答應觀眾公平待遇的芮安。稍後兩人一起收椅子時，他承認自己過去曾把意見一致誤當成好感。芮安告訴他，她更喜歡能好好爭論、也願意聽的人。兩人都沒許下宏大的誓言；下一站還會考驗他們。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "sack", "sorrow", "steam", and "violent". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A listener remembered sack from an ordinary day. One card about sorrow brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed steam through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word violent described their memory fairly. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著sack（袋子）、sorrow（悲傷）、steam（蒸氣）、violent（猛烈的）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「袋子」。一張關於「悲傷」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「蒸氣」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「猛烈的」，並說明原因。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at a converted repair garage?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. A promotional recording used a grandmother's voice without permission.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：一段宣傳錄音未經同意使用了祖母的聲音。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. Rui replaced the recording with live narration written by volunteers.
B. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安改用志工共同撰寫的現場口述。

## CHAPTER 06｜A Light That Was Not a Star｜不是星星的亮光
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the farming town of South Field while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until an unusual light had started rumors that the show was hiding a secret weapon. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在南田農業小鎮時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：一道異常亮光引發傳聞，說展覽藏有祕密武器。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "anyhow", "assume", "bacteria", and "bump". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One note included anyhow and left the final question open. One speaker asked how to assume in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about bacteria in a story about the town. A listener remembered bump from an ordinary day. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著anyhow（總之）、assume（假設）、bacteria（細菌）、bump（碰撞）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張便條寫上「總之」，卻保留最後的問題。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「假設」。一名鄰居把「細菌」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「碰撞」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "creator", "excellence", "inner", and "missile". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed creator through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about excellence in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly inner. One card about missile brought two different memories together. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是creator（創作者）、excellence（卓越）、inner（內部的）、missile（飛彈）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「創作者」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「卓越」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「內部的」。一張關於「飛彈」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: a flight notice and careful timing identified an ordinary aircraft. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Rui invited a science teacher to explain how people test a claim. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：一份飛航公告與精確時間比對指出那只是普通飛機。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，芮安邀請科學教師示範如何檢驗一項說法。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "multiply", "necessity", "politician", and "resource". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A listener asked what it meant to multiply in an everyday choice. A listener remembered necessity from an ordinary day. One card about politician brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed resource through a concrete detail, not a guess. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：multiply（使倍增）、necessity（必要）、politician（政治人物）、resource（資源）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「使倍增」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「必要」。一張關於「政治人物」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「資源」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "stale", "stomach", "summary", and "suspect". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly stale. One card about stomach brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed summary through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about suspect in a story about the town. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著stale（不新鮮的）、stomach（胃）、summary（摘要）、suspect（嫌疑人）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「不新鮮的」。一張關於「胃」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「摘要」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「嫌疑人」放入小鎮故事。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: a local politician wanted a dramatic speech instead of a demonstration. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, she gave the teacher the microphone and let evidence do the work. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：一名地方政治人物希望發表戲劇化演說而非進行示範。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：她把麥克風交給教師，讓證據自己說話。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "technique", "teenage", "tropical", and "wax". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One card about technique brought two different memories together. A visitor said teenage did not mean the same thing to every family. A speaker asked whether the word tropical described their memory fairly. A listener remembered wax from an ordinary day. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著technique（技巧）、teenage（青少年的）、tropical（熱帶的）、wax（蠟）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一張關於「技巧」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「青少年的」對每個家庭不一定相同。有人稱某段回憶是「熱帶的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「蠟」。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

## CHAPTER 07｜The Night of the Battery｜電池出問題的那一夜
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the brick school courtyard, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: the backup battery began to smell hot during setup. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在磚造學校中庭，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：備用電池在架設時開始散發熱味。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "appeal", "awful", "awkward", and "brick". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed appeal through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word awful described their memory fairly. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly awkward. One card about brick brought two different memories together. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是appeal（呼籲）、awful（糟糕的）、awkward（彆扭的）、brick（磚塊）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「呼籲」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「糟糕的」，並說明原因。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「彆扭的」。一張關於「磚塊」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "cupboard", "dime", "emergency", and "explode". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A neighbor talked about cupboard in a story about the town. A listener remembered dime from an ordinary day. One card about emergency brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to explode in a fair and careful way. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：cupboard（櫥櫃）、dime（十美分）、emergency（緊急狀況）、explode（爆發）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名鄰居把「櫥櫃」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「十美分」。一張關於「緊急狀況」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「爆發」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: a gauge showed rising heat before anyone saw smoke. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Jonas shut off the unit and guided everyone to the outdoor meeting point. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當儀表在任何人看見煙霧前就顯示溫度上升，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，喬森關閉設備並引導所有人到戶外集合點。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "flavor", "flesh", "invent", and "jar". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A listener remembered flavor from an ordinary day. One card about flesh brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to invent in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about jar in a story about the town. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著flavor（氣味）、flesh（肉體／有血有肉的人）、invent（發明）、jar（罐子）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「氣味」。一張關於「肉體／有血有肉的人」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「發明」。一名鄰居把「罐子」放入小鎮故事。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "junk", "lily", "remind", and "sometime". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One card about junk brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed lily through a concrete detail, not a guess. A listener asked what it meant to remind in an everyday choice. An older neighbor chose sometime to explain how the story unfolded. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著junk（垃圾）、lily（百合）、remind（提醒）、sometime（改天）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一張關於「垃圾」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「百合」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「提醒」。一位長者選用「改天」解釋事情如何發生。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that a sponsor representative urged them to hide the failed test. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: Rui recorded the interruption and canceled the show until an inspection. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：贊助商代表催促他們隱瞞失敗的測試。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：芮安記錄中斷原因，取消演出直到檢查完成。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "steady", "terrific", "vase", and "wage". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A visitor said steady did not mean the same thing to every family. A speaker asked whether the word terrific described their memory fairly. A listener remembered vase from an ordinary day. One card about wage brought two different memories together. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著steady（穩定的）、terrific（極好的）、vase（花瓶）、wage（工資）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名訪客說「穩定的」對每個家庭不一定相同。有人稱某段回憶是「極好的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「花瓶」。一張關於「工資」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the brick school courtyard?**
A. The backup battery began to smell hot during setup.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：備用電池在架設時開始散發熱味。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Jonas shut off the unit and guided everyone to the outdoor meeting point.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：喬森關閉設備並引導所有人到戶外集合點。

## CHAPTER 08｜The Right to Leave a Story｜說故事也能說不要
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached the village reading room, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that a recorded interview included details its speaker no longer wished to share. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達村莊閱覽室時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：一段訪談包含講述者已不願分享的細節。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "baggage", "buffalo", "bullet", and "citizen". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A neighbor talked about baggage in a story about the town. A listener remembered buffalo from an ordinary day. One card about bullet brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed citizen through a concrete detail, not a guess. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：baggage（行李）、buffalo（水牛）、bullet（項目符號）、citizen（市民）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名鄰居把「行李」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「水牛」。一張關於「項目符號」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「市民」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "controller", "cooker", "crispy", and "grab". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A listener remembered controller from an ordinary day. One card about cooker brought two different memories together. A visitor said crispy did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about grab in a story about the town. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著controller（控制器）、cooker（電鍋）、crispy（酥脆的）、grab（抓住）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「控制器」。一張關於「電鍋」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「酥脆的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「抓住」放入小鎮故事。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: a permission form had been signed before the speaker understood the public website. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui removed the recording from the online queue and offered a private review. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：同意書是在講述者弄清公開網站用途之前簽的。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安從上線佇列撤下錄音，改安排私下確認。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "lately", "litter", "owe", and "permission". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One note included lately and left the final question open. A volunteer discussed litter through a concrete detail, not a guess. A listener asked what it meant to owe in an everyday choice. A listener remembered permission from an ordinary day. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著lately（最近）、litter（垃圾）、owe（欠（某人））、permission（許可）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一張便條寫上「最近」，卻保留最後的問題。志工把「垃圾」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「欠（某人）」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「許可」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "process", "request", "sexual", and "shadow". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A volunteer discussed process through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about request in a story about the town. A card about sexual privacy went to an adults-only discussion of consent. One card about shadow brought two different memories together. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著process（過程）、request（請求）、sexual（性的（性健康））、shadow（陰影）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。志工把「過程」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「請求」放入小鎮故事。一張關於性隱私的卡片被安排在僅限成人的同意權討論。一張關於「陰影」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Success was still uncertain. The sharpest objection was that the sponsor complained that fewer clips would lower its statistics. Rui did not dismiss the worry merely because it came from someone powerful. She separated its real cost from the demand hidden inside it. Then she changed the measure of success from uploads to informed choices. Jonas helped with tools and timings, but he left the final public explanation to the person who had promised the audience a fair program. Later, when they folded the chairs together, he admitted that he had once mistaken agreement for affection. Rui told him she preferred a partner who could argue carefully and still listen. Neither offered a grand promise. The next stop would test them both.

**繁中**
事情是否成功仍不確定。最尖銳的反對聲是：贊助商抱怨片段減少會拉低統計數字。芮安沒有因為這話來自權勢者就直接否定，而是把真正的成本與藏在其中的要求分開。然後，她把成功的衡量標準從上傳數量改成知情選擇。喬森協助工具與時程，卻把公開說明留給曾答應觀眾公平待遇的芮安。稍後兩人一起收椅子時，他承認自己過去曾把意見一致誤當成好感。芮安告訴他，她更喜歡能好好爭論、也願意聽的人。兩人都沒許下宏大的誓言；下一站還會考驗他們。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "talkative", "wealthy", "web", and "yell". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A speaker asked whether the word talkative described their memory fairly. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly wealthy. One card about web brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed yell through a concrete detail, not a guess. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是talkative（健談的）、wealthy（富有的）、web（網路）、yell（大喊）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。有人稱某段回憶是「健談的」，並說明原因。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「富有的」。一張關於「網路」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「大喊」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：一段訪談包含講述者已不願分享的細節。

**Answer: A**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安從上線佇列撤下錄音，改安排私下確認。

## CHAPTER 09｜A Child Beside the Tent｜帳篷旁的孩子
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the first fully public night show while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until a boy became separated from his older sister near the food stalls. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在第一場完整對外開放的夜間展時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：一個男孩在攤位附近和姊姊走散。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "bleed", "bore", "cabin", and "database". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. Someone asked when it would be right to bleed. One card about bore brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed cabin through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about database in a story about the town. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著bleed（滲入）、bore（使感到無聊）、cabin（小木屋）、database（資料庫）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有人思考什麼時候該「滲入」，又該何時停下。一張關於「使感到無聊」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「小木屋」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「資料庫」放入小鎮故事。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "democracy", "faint", "fright", and "horn". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One card about democracy brought two different memories together. A visitor said faint did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about fright in a story about the town. A listener remembered horn from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著democracy（民主）、faint（微弱的／淡淡的）、fright（驚恐）、horn（喇叭）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一張關於「民主」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「微弱的／淡淡的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「驚恐」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「喇叭」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: a note at the entrance named the family's agreed meeting place. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Rui paused the program and asked trained volunteers to guide the search. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：入口的一張便條記著家人約定的集合處。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，芮安暫停節目，請受過訓練的志工引導尋人。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "laughter", "lover", "missing", and "roar". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A volunteer discussed laughter through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about lover in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly missing. One card about roar brought two different memories together. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著laughter（笑聲）、lover（戀人）、missing（遺失的）、roar（吼叫）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。志工把「笑聲」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「戀人」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「遺失的」。一張關於「吼叫」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "shortly", "sink", "skillful", and "splash". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A visitor used shortly while describing the order of events. A listener remembered sink from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really skillful and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed splash through a concrete detail, not a guess. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是shortly（很快地）、sink（下沉）、skillful（熟練的）、splash（濺起水花）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一名訪客用「很快地」說明事件的先後。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「下沉」。志工用「熟練的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「濺起水花」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: a loud promotional horn made the instructions hard to hear. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, she required a quiet emergency channel before the next event. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：響亮的宣傳喇叭讓指示難以聽清。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：她要求下一場活動前設立安靜的緊急聯絡管道。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "tune", "wander", "weapon", and "weekly". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A listener remembered tune from an ordinary day. One card about wander brought two different memories together. The weapon card was kept in a fictional story about fear, not a threat. A speaker asked whether the word weekly described their memory fairly. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：tune（調整頻率）、wander（閒逛）、weapon（武器）、weekly（每週的）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「調整頻率」。一張關於「閒逛」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。武器卡片被用在談論恐懼的虛構故事裡，而不是威脅。有人稱某段回憶是「每週的」，並說明原因。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the first fully public night show?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
C. A boy became separated from his older sister near the food stalls.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：一個男孩在攤位附近和姊姊走散。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. Rui paused the program and asked trained volunteers to guide the search.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安暫停節目，請受過訓練的志工引導尋人。

## CHAPTER 10｜The Sea Behind the Stars｜星空後面的海
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the coastal wedding garden, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: the evening tide threatened a narrow audience path. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在海岸婚禮花園，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：傍晚漲潮威脅著狹窄的觀眾通道。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "angel", "bold", "bride", and "determine". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One card about angel brought two different memories together. A visitor said bold did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about bride in a story about the town. Someone asked when it would be right to determine. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著angel（天使）、bold（大膽的）、bride（新娘）、determine（決定）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一張關於「天使」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「大膽的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「新娘」放入小鎮故事。有人思考什麼時候該「決定」，又該何時停下。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "fashionable", "flame", "handkerchief", and "impress". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A visitor said fashionable did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about flame in a story about the town. A listener remembered handkerchief from an ordinary day. A volunteer used impress in an example about responsibility. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著fashionable（時髦的）、flame（火焰）、handkerchief（手帕）、impress（留下印象）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名訪客說「時髦的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「火焰」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「手帕」。一名志工用「留下印象」舉出責任的例子。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: a local fisher's tide chart showed the danger hours before dark. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui moved the dome uphill and kept the ceremony separate from the public show. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當當地漁民的潮汐表早在天黑前數小時就顯示危險，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安把星空帳篷移上坡地，讓婚禮與公共展覽各自進行。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "melon", "moral", "optimistic", and "pearl". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A neighbor talked about melon in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly moral. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really optimistic and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed pearl through a concrete detail, not a guess. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是melon（甜瓜）、moral（道德的）、optimistic（樂觀的）、pearl（珍珠）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一名鄰居把「甜瓜」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「道德的」。志工用「樂觀的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「珍珠」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "punch", "relief", "silk", and "somehow". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A listener remembered punch from an ordinary day. One card about relief brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed silk through a concrete detail, not a guess. A visitor used somehow while describing the order of events. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：punch（揮拳）、relief（寬慰）、silk（絲綢）、somehow（不知何故）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「揮拳」。一張關於「寬慰」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「絲綢」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名訪客用「不知何故」說明事件的先後。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that a wedding planner wanted a beautiful picture on the beach regardless of risk. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: she chose a less fashionable place where everyone could leave safely. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：婚禮策劃者不顧風險，仍想在沙灘拍出漂亮照片。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：她選擇不那麼時髦、但所有人都能安全離開的地點。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "stare", "stove", "structure", and "tide". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. One card about stare brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed stove through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about structure in a story about the town. A listener remembered tide from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著stare（盯著看）、stove（爐子）、structure（結構）、tide（潮汐）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一張關於「盯著看」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「爐子」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「結構」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「潮汐」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

| L3-0856 | `structure` | n. | `structure` | 結構 | A neighbor talked about structure in a story about the town. |
| L3-0910 | `tide` | n. | `tide` | 潮汐 | Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: the evening tide threatened a narrow audience path. |

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the coastal wedding garden?**
A. The evening tide threatened a narrow audience path.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：傍晚漲潮威脅著狹窄的觀眾通道。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Rui moved the dome uphill and kept the ceremony separate from the public show.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安把星空帳篷移上坡地，讓婚禮與公共展覽各自進行。
