## CHAPTER 11｜The Hill Without Power｜沒有電的山丘
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached the highland bus stop, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that the town's electrical supply could not safely support a full show. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達高地公車站時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：小鎮供電無法安全支援完整展演。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "accurate", "bless", "county", and "ditch". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A visitor said accurate did not mean the same thing to every family. A listener asked what it meant to bless in an everyday choice. A listener remembered county from an ordinary day. One card about ditch brought two different memories together. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著accurate（精確的）、bless（祝福）、county（郡）、ditch（溝渠）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名訪客說「精確的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「祝福」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「郡」。一張關於「溝渠」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "dive", "freezer", "gasoline", and "intelligent". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A neighbor talked about dive in a story about the town. A listener remembered freezer from an ordinary day. One card about gasoline brought two different memories together. A visitor said intelligent did not mean the same thing to every family. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是dive（跳水）、freezer（冷凍庫）、gasoline（汽油）、intelligent（聰明的）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一名鄰居把「跳水」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「冷凍庫」。一張關於「汽油」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「聰明的」對每個家庭不一定相同。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: measurements proved that a small lamp and spoken stories needed far less power. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Jonas built a low-power version while Rui rehearsed a show without moving images. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：測量結果證明小燈與口述故事需要的電力少得多。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，喬森製作低耗電版本，芮安排練不使用動態影像的節目。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "loose", "medium", "muscle", and "operation". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly loose. One card about medium brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed muscle through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about operation in a story about the town. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：loose（鬆動的）、medium（媒體）、muscle（肌肉）、operation（操作／作業）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「鬆動的」。一張關於「媒體」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「肌肉」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「操作／作業」放入小鎮故事。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "perform", "plastic", "roughly", and "sweat". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A volunteer used perform in an example about responsibility. A volunteer discussed plastic through a concrete detail, not a guess. A visitor used roughly while describing the order of events. A listener remembered sweat from an ordinary day. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著perform（運作／表現）、plastic（塑膠）、roughly（大約）、sweat（汗）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名志工用「運作／表現」舉出責任的例子。志工把「塑膠」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名訪客用「大約」說明事件的先後。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「汗」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
Success was still uncertain. The sharpest objection was that a news reporter expected the reduced program to disappoint everyone. Rui did not dismiss the worry merely because it came from someone powerful. She separated its real cost from the demand hidden inside it. Then Rui asked the audience what they learned rather than claiming a perfect performance. Jonas helped with tools and timings, but he left the final public explanation to the person who had promised the audience a fair program. Later, when they folded the chairs together, he admitted that he had once mistaken agreement for affection. Rui told him she preferred a partner who could argue carefully and still listen. Neither offered a grand promise. The next stop would test them both.

**繁中**
事情是否成功仍不確定。最尖銳的反對聲是：一位新聞記者以為縮減設備必定讓所有人失望。芮安沒有因為這話來自權勢者就直接否定，而是把真正的成本與藏在其中的要求分開。然後，芮安詢問觀眾學到了什麼，而不宣稱演出完美。喬森協助工具與時程，卻把公開說明留給曾答應觀眾公平待遇的芮安。稍後兩人一起收椅子時，他承認自己過去曾把意見一致誤當成好感。芮安告訴他，她更喜歡能好好爭論、也願意聽的人。兩人都沒許下宏大的誓言；下一站還會考驗他們。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "theory", "timber", "violet", and "weaken". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A volunteer discussed theory through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about timber in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly violet. A volunteer used weaken in an example about responsibility. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著theory（理論）、timber（木板）、violet（紫羅蘭（色））、weaken（削弱）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。志工把「理論」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「木板」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「紫羅蘭（色）」。一名志工用「削弱」舉出責任的例子。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

## CHAPTER 12｜The Canyon Letters｜峽谷裡的信
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the canyon community hall while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until the hall owner demanded exclusive control of all visitor messages. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在峽谷社區活動中心時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：活動中心所有人要求獨家控制所有觀眾留言。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "aside", "bubble", "canyon", and "capable". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A visitor used aside while describing the order of events. A listener remembered bubble from an ordinary day. One card about canyon brought two different memories together. A visitor said capable did not mean the same thing to every family. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是aside（在一旁）、bubble（氣泡）、canyon（峽谷）、capable（有能力的）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一名訪客用「在一旁」說明事件的先後。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「氣泡」。一張關於「峽谷」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「有能力的」對每個家庭不一定相同。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "captain", "client", "edit", and "elderly". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A listener remembered captain from an ordinary day. One card about client brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to edit in a fair and careful way. A speaker asked whether the word elderly described their memory fairly. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：captain（船長）、client（委託人）、edit（編輯）、elderly（年長的）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「船長」。一張關於「委託人」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「編輯」。有人稱某段回憶是「年長的」，並說明原因。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: old letters showed the building had been donated for public education. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Rui asked the town council to read the property agreement in public. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：舊信件顯示這棟建築原是捐給公共教育用途。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，芮安請鎮議會公開宣讀財產使用協議。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "electricity", "flash", "hatch", and "hopeful". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. One card about electricity brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed flash through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about hatch in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly hopeful. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著electricity（電力）、flash（發明）、hatch（艙口）、hopeful（有希望的）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一張關於「電力」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「發明」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「艙口」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「有希望的」。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "information", "monthly", "property", and "represent". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A volunteer discussed information through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word monthly described their memory fairly. A listener remembered property from an ordinary day. A volunteer used represent in an example about responsibility. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著information（資訊）、monthly（每月的）、property（財產）、represent（代表）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。志工把「資訊」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「每月的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「財產」。一名志工用「代表」舉出責任的例子。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: the owner warned that arguing might end the entire visit. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, she chose a smaller temporary venue while the disagreement was resolved. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：所有人警告爭論可能讓整場巡演無法舉辦。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：她選擇較小的臨時場地，讓爭議繼續按程序處理。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "situation", "spice", "sword", and "weep". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A neighbor talked about situation in a story about the town. A listener remembered spice from an ordinary day. One card about sword brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to weep in a fair and careful way. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著situation（情況）、spice（香料）、sword（長劍）、weep（哭泣）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名鄰居把「情況」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「香料」。一張關於「長劍」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「哭泣」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the canyon community hall?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
C. The hall owner demanded exclusive control of all visitor messages.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：活動中心所有人要求獨家控制所有觀眾留言。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. Rui asked the town council to read the property agreement in public.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安請鎮議會公開宣讀財產使用協議。

## CHAPTER 13｜The Puppet That Looked Away｜轉過頭的木偶
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the old market theater, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: a puppet show repeated a hurtful joke about people from another town. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在舊市場劇場，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：木偶劇重複了嘲笑另一個鎮居民的傷人笑話。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "assistant", "attractive", "barely", and "barrel". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A listener remembered assistant from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really attractive and welcomed another view. A student tried barely in a sentence about their journey. A neighbor talked about barrel in a story about the town. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：assistant（助理）、attractive（吸引人的）、barely（幾乎不）、barrel（桶）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「助理」。志工用「吸引人的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。一名學生在旅途句子中試著使用「幾乎不」。一名鄰居把「桶」放入小鎮故事。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "bitter", "comparison", "crane", and "gang". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really bitter and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed comparison through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about crane in a story about the town. A listener remembered gang from an ordinary day. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著bitter（苦澀的）、comparison（比較）、crane（起重機）、gang（一群人）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。志工用「苦澀的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「比較」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「起重機」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「一群人」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: the original script contained a gentler scene that had been removed. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui invited the affected performers to decide how the scene should change. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當原劇本原有一場較溫和的戲，後來遭人刪除，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安邀請受到影響的表演者決定如何修改那場戲。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "grasp", "marble", "mosquito", and "pea". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A volunteer discussed grasp through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about marble in a story about the town. A listener remembered mosquito from an ordinary day. One card about pea brought two different memories together. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著grasp（理解）、marble（大理石）、mosquito（蚊子）、pea（豌豆）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。志工把「理解」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「大理石」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「蚊子」。一張關於「豌豆」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "puppet", "rate", "satisfactory", and "stubborn". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A neighbor talked about puppet in a story about the town. A listener remembered rate from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really satisfactory and welcomed another view. A visitor said stubborn did not mean the same thing to every family. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著puppet（玩偶）、rate（評分）、satisfactory（令人滿意的）、stubborn（固執的）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名鄰居把「玩偶」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「評分」。志工用「令人滿意的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。一名訪客說「固執的」對每個家庭不一定相同。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that the director insisted that tradition could never be questioned. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: the cast agreed on a new ending without erasing its history. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：導演堅稱傳統絕對不能受到質疑。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：全體演員同意新的結尾，同時不抹除這段歷史。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "tailor", "trumpet", "underlying", and "vary". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A listener remembered tailor from an ordinary day. One card about trumpet brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to underlying in a fair and careful way. A listener asked what it meant to vary in an everyday choice. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是tailor（裁縫）、trumpet（小號）、underlying（根本的）、vary（變化）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「裁縫」。一張關於「小號」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「根本的」。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「變化」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

A. A puppet show repeated a hurtful joke about people from another town.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：木偶劇重複了嘲笑另一個鎮居民的傷人笑話。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Rui invited the affected performers to decide how the scene should change.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安邀請受到影響的表演者決定如何修改那場戲。

## CHAPTER 14｜The Hearing in the Rain｜雨中的公開聽證
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached the county public meeting room, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that officials questioned whether free shows were worth their travel costs. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達郡政府公共會議室時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：官員質疑免費演出是否值得交通成本。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "announce", "apart", "brunch", and "creature". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. One card about announce brought two different memories together. A student tried apart in a sentence about their journey. A neighbor talked about brunch in a story about the town. A listener remembered creature from an ordinary day. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著announce（宣布／公告）、apart（分開）、brunch（早午餐）、creature（生物）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一張關於「宣布／公告」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名學生在旅途句子中試著使用「分開」。一名鄰居把「早午餐」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「生物」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "enable", "export", "fare", and "fighter". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One speaker asked how to enable in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about export in a story about the town. A listener remembered fare from an ordinary day. One card about fighter brought two different memories together. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著enable（使能夠）、export（出口）、fare（車資）、fighter（拳擊手）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「使能夠」。一名鄰居把「出口」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「車資」。一張關於「拳擊手」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: receipts showed that rural families otherwise had no nearby cultural program. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui presented costs, access records, and examples of audience consent. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：收據顯示偏鄉家庭附近原本沒有其他文化活動。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安提出成本、參與紀錄與觀眾同意的實例。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "indoor", "jail", "ladder", and "nickname". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A speaker asked whether the word indoor described their memory fairly. A listener remembered jail from an ordinary day. One card about ladder brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed nickname through a concrete detail, not a guess. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著indoor（室內的）、jail（監獄）、ladder（梯子）、nickname（綽號）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。有人稱某段回憶是「室內的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「監獄」。一張關於「梯子」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「綽號」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "peel", "pity", "racial", and "robbery". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A listener remembered peel from an ordinary day. One card about pity brought two different memories together. The word racial led the adults to examine unfair assumptions in a script. A child used robbery in an invented detective story rather than naming a real suspect. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是peel（剝皮）、pity（憐憫）、racial（種族的）、robbery（搶劫）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「剝皮」。一張關於「憐憫」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。種族相關一詞讓成年人檢查劇本中的不公平假設。孩子把搶劫放進虛構的偵探故事，而沒有點名真實嫌疑人。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Success was still uncertain. The sharpest objection was that a business group suggested charging more to make the event seem valuable. Rui did not dismiss the worry merely because it came from someone powerful. She separated its real cost from the demand hidden inside it. Then she requested a public vote on a transparent funding plan. Jonas helped with tools and timings, but he left the final public explanation to the person who had promised the audience a fair program. Later, when they folded the chairs together, he admitted that he had once mistaken agreement for affection. Rui told him she preferred a partner who could argue carefully and still listen. Neither offered a grand promise. The next stop would test them both.

**繁中**
事情是否成功仍不確定。最尖銳的反對聲是：一個商業團體建議提高收費以讓活動看起來更有價值。芮安沒有因為這話來自權勢者就直接否定，而是把真正的成本與藏在其中的要求分開。然後，她要求對透明的資助計畫進行公開表決。喬森協助工具與時程，卻把公開說明留給曾答應觀眾公平待遇的芮安。稍後兩人一起收椅子時，他承認自己過去曾把意見一致誤當成好感。芮安告訴他，她更喜歡能好好爭論、也願意聽的人。兩人都沒許下宏大的誓言；下一站還會考驗他們。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "slippery", "suspicion", "tourism", and "urban". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really slippery and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed suspicion through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about tourism in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly urban. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：slippery（滑的）、suspicion（懷疑）、tourism（觀光業）、urban（都市的）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工用「滑的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「懷疑」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「觀光業」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「都市的」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the county public meeting room?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. Officials questioned whether free shows were worth their travel costs.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：官員質疑免費演出是否值得交通成本。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. Rui presented costs, access records, and examples of audience consent.
B. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安提出成本、參與紀錄與觀眾同意的實例。

## CHAPTER 15｜The Dome in Heavy Rain｜大雨裡的星空帳篷
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the roadside indoor gym while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until heavy rain drove water beneath the portable dome. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在路旁的室內體育館時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：大雨讓水滲入移動式星空帳篷底下。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "breast", "buzz", "carriage", and "conclusion". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A volunteer discussed breast through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about buzz in a story about the town. A listener remembered carriage from an ordinary day. One card about conclusion brought two different memories together. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著breast（乳房）、buzz（嗡嗡聲）、carriage（馬車）、conclusion（結論）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。志工把「乳房」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「嗡嗡聲」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「馬車」。一張關於「結論」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "crew", "global", "jewel", and "mobile". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A neighbor talked about crew in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly global. One card about jewel brought two different memories together. A visitor said mobile did not mean the same thing to every family. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著crew（工作人員）、global（全球的）、jewel（寶石）、mobile（可移動的）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名鄰居把「工作人員」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「全球的」。一張關於「寶石」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「可移動的」對每個家庭不一定相同。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: a moisture sensor warned them before the projector was damaged. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Jonas dismantled the wet equipment while Rui turned the program into a story circle. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：濕度感測器在投影機受損前就發出警告。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，喬森拆卸潮濕設備，芮安把節目改成故事分享圈。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "moisture", "mostly", "pancake", and "pause". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A listener remembered moisture from an ordinary day. One note included mostly and left the final question open. A volunteer discussed pancake through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about pause in a story about the town. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是moisture（濕氣）、mostly（大多數）、pancake（鬆餅）、pause（暫停）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「濕氣」。一張便條寫上「大多數」，卻保留最後的問題。志工把「鬆餅」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「暫停」放入小鎮故事。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "pill", "recognize", "remain", and "scale". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. One card about pill brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to recognize in a fair and careful way. A listener asked what it meant to remain in an everyday choice. A listener remembered scale from an ordinary day. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：pill（藥丸）、recognize（認出）、remain（留下）、scale（秤）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一張關於「藥丸」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「認出」。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「留下」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「秤」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: the rushed change left several visitors without clear directions. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, they apologized, improved signs, and practiced the new indoor plan. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：匆忙更改讓幾名訪客不清楚方向。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：兩人道歉、改善標示，並排練新的室內方案。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "surround", "tasty", "temporary", and "tug". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. One speaker asked how to surround in a fair and careful way. A speaker asked whether the word tasty described their memory fairly. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly temporary. One card about tug brought two different memories together. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著surround（環繞）、tasty（美味的）、temporary（暫時的）、tug（拉扯）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「環繞」。有人稱某段回憶是「美味的」，並說明原因。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「暫時的」。一張關於「拉扯」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**Answer: B**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：喬森拆卸潮濕設備，芮安把節目改成故事分享圈。

## CHAPTER 16｜A Summit Covered by Cloud｜雲裡的山頂
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the mountain summit shelter, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: clouds hid every star on the evening they had promised a sky lesson. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在山頂避難小屋，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：承諾進行星空課的夜晚，雲層卻遮住所有星星。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "confirm", "fairly", "flood", and "fond". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A listener asked what it meant to confirm in an everyday choice. An older neighbor chose fairly to explain how the story unfolded. One card about flood brought two different memories together. A visitor said fond did not mean the same thing to every family. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著confirm（確認）、fairly（相當地）、flood（洪水）、fond（喜愛的）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「確認」。一位長者選用「相當地」解釋事情如何發生。一張關於「洪水」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「喜愛的」對每個家庭不一定相同。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "gap", "graduate", "grin", and "honor". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A listener remembered gap from an ordinary day. One card about graduate brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed grin through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about honor in a story about the town. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是gap（缺口）、graduate（畢業生）、grin（露齒笑）、honor（遵守）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「缺口」。一張關於「畢業生」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「露齒笑」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「遵守」放入小鎮故事。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: a blind student suggested using raised lines and sound instead of pictures. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui tested a touchable star map with volunteers before presenting it. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當一位視障學生建議用凸線與聲音代替圖像，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安先與志工測試可觸摸星圖，才拿給大家使用。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "mystery", "neat", "observe", and "parrot". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. One card about mystery brought two different memories together. A visitor said neat did not mean the same thing to every family. A listener asked what it meant to observe in an everyday choice. A listener remembered parrot from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：mystery（謎團）、neat（整齊的）、observe（觀察）、parrot（鸚鵡）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一張關於「謎團」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「整齊的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「觀察」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「鸚鵡」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "patience", "preparation", "rely", and "significant". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A volunteer discussed patience through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about preparation in a story about the town. Someone asked when it would be right to rely. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really significant and welcomed another view. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著patience（耐心）、preparation（準備）、rely（依靠）、significant（重要的）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。志工把「耐心」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「準備」放入小鎮故事。有人思考什麼時候該「依靠」，又該何時停下。志工用「重要的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that a sponsor feared a quiet lesson would not look impressive in photographs. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: she kept the tactile plan and asked the student to critique it afterward. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：贊助商擔心安靜的課程拍起照來不夠壯觀。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：她保留觸覺方案，並請那名學生事後提出批評。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "slave", "summit", "swift", and "vitamin". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. The word slave opened a careful discussion of historical injustice. A listener remembered summit from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really swift and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed vitamin through a concrete detail, not a guess. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著slave（奴隸）、summit（山頂）、swift（迅速的）、vitamin（維他命）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。奴隸一詞引發了一段對歷史不公的審慎討論。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「山頂」。志工用「迅速的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「維他命」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the mountain summit shelter?**
A. Clouds hid every star on the evening they had promised a sky lesson.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：承諾進行星空課的夜晚，雲層卻遮住所有星星。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Rui tested a touchable star map with volunteers before presenting it.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安先與志工測試可觸摸星圖，才拿給大家使用。

## CHAPTER 17｜What the Factory Street Remembered｜工廠街記得什麼
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached the industrial river district, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that a former worker asked that a painful family story stay private. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達工業河岸區時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：一名前工人要求一段痛苦的家庭故事保持私人性。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "aware", "badly", "bury", and "cone". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly aware. One note included badly and left the final question open. One speaker asked how to bury in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about cone in a story about the town. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是aware（知道）、badly（迫切地）、bury（埋入）、cone（交通錐）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「知道」。一張便條寫上「迫切地」，卻保留最後的問題。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「埋入」。一名鄰居把「交通錐」放入小鎮故事。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "dolphin", "elbow", "expectation", and "frequent". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. One card about dolphin brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed elbow through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about expectation in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly frequent. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：dolphin（海豚）、elbow（手肘）、expectation（期待）、frequent（頻繁的）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一張關於「海豚」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「手肘」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「期待」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「頻繁的」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: an interview summary had confused community history with an individual's medical history. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui removed identifying details and consulted the speaker about a different scene. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：訪談摘要把社區歷史和個人的醫療經歷混在一起。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安去掉足以辨識身分的細節，和講述者商量改用另一段情節。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "fur", "industrial", "inspect", and "journey". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A volunteer discussed fur through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word industrial described their memory fairly. Someone asked when it would be right to inspect. One card about journey brought two different memories together. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著fur（毛皮）、industrial（工業的）、inspect（檢查）、journey（旅程）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。志工把「毛皮」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「工業的」，並說明原因。有人思考什麼時候該「檢查」，又該何時停下。一張關於「旅程」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "lively", "mayor", "producer", and "rot". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A speaker asked whether the word lively described their memory fairly. A listener remembered mayor from an ordinary day. One card about producer brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed rot through a concrete detail, not a guess. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著lively（活潑的）、mayor（市長）、producer（製作人）、rot（腐爛）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。有人稱某段回憶是「活潑的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「市長」。一張關於「製作人」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「腐爛」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Success was still uncertain. The sharpest objection was that the mayor's publicity team asked for a dramatic personal story. Rui did not dismiss the worry merely because it came from someone powerful. She separated its real cost from the demand hidden inside it. Then she refused to turn suffering into a ticket for publicity. Jonas helped with tools and timings, but he left the final public explanation to the person who had promised the audience a fair program. Later, when they folded the chairs together, he admitted that he had once mistaken agreement for affection. Rui told him she preferred a partner who could argue carefully and still listen. Neither offered a grand promise. The next stop would test them both.

**繁中**
事情是否成功仍不確定。最尖銳的反對聲是：市長的宣傳團隊要求一段有戲劇性的個人故事。芮安沒有因為這話來自權勢者就直接否定，而是把真正的成本與藏在其中的要求分開。然後，她拒絕把別人的痛苦當成宣傳的入場券。喬森協助工具與時程，卻把公開說明留給曾答應觀眾公平待遇的芮安。稍後兩人一起收椅子時，他承認自己過去曾把意見一致誤當成好感。芮安告訴他，她更喜歡能好好爭論、也願意聽的人。兩人都沒許下宏大的誓言；下一站還會考驗他們。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "sauce", "suicide", "tent", and "whip". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A listener remembered sauce from an ordinary day. One card raised suicide, and Rui offered a private conversation instead of a public performance. A volunteer discussed tent through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about whip in a story about the town. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著sauce（醬汁）、suicide（自殺）、tent（帳篷）、whip（鞭子）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「醬汁」。有一張卡提到自殺，芮安改提供私下談話，不讓它成為公開表演。志工把「帳篷」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「鞭子」放入小鎮故事。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

### 本章新目標詞

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the industrial river district?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. A former worker asked that a painful family story stay private.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：一名前工人要求一段痛苦的家庭故事保持私人性。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. Rui removed identifying details and consulted the speaker about a different scene.
B. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安去掉足以辨識身分的細節，和講述者商量改用另一段情節。

## CHAPTER 18｜The Forest That Needed Darkness｜需要黑暗的森林
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the northern woodland reserve while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until bright exhibition lights disturbed animals near the field station. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在北方林地保護區時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：明亮的展場燈光干擾了野外工作站附近的動物。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "ambassador", "bare", "colony", and "drugstore". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. One card about ambassador brought two different memories together. A visitor said bare did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about colony in a story about the town. A listener remembered drugstore from an ordinary day. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：ambassador（大使）、bare（空蕩的）、colony（群落）、drugstore（藥局）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一張關於「大使」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「空蕩的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「群落」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「藥局」。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "exchange", "experiment", "fold", and "hateful". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A volunteer discussed exchange through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about experiment in a story about the town. A listener remembered fold from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really hateful and welcomed another view. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著exchange（交換）、experiment（實驗）、fold（摺疊）、hateful（充滿仇恨的）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。志工把「交換」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「實驗」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「摺疊」。志工用「充滿仇恨的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: a ranger showed how moths changed their flight around the lamps. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Jonas reduced the lighting and Rui taught visitors to listen without wandering off trails. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：一位巡護員示範飛蛾如何因燈光改變飛行方向。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，喬森減少照明，芮安教訪客在不離開步道的情況下傾聽。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "horror", "indoors", "mushroom", and "opposite". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A neighbor talked about horror in a story about the town. An older neighbor chose indoors to explain how the story unfolded. One card about mushroom brought two different memories together. A visitor said opposite did not mean the same thing to every family. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著horror（恐怖）、indoors（在室內）、mushroom（蘑菇）、opposite（對面的）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一名鄰居把「恐怖」放入小鎮故事。一位長者選用「在室內」解釋事情如何發生。一張關於「蘑菇」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「對面的」對每個家庭不一定相同。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "pat", "routine", "shorten", and "statue". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A listener remembered pat from an ordinary day. One card about routine brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to shorten in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about statue in a story about the town. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著pat（輕拍）、routine（例行程序）、shorten（縮短）、statue（雕像）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「輕拍」。一張關於「例行程序」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「縮短」。一名鄰居把「雕像」放入小鎮故事。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: an advertiser wanted a huge glowing sign at the forest entrance. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, they held a quiet walk and canceled the advertising sign. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：廣告商想在森林入口豎起巨大發光招牌。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：他們改辦安靜的步行活動，取消那面廣告招牌。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "sticky", "survivor", "transport", and "weed". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really sticky and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed survivor through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about transport in a story about the town. A listener remembered weed from an ordinary day. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是sticky（黏的）、survivor（倖存者）、transport（運輸）、weed（雜草）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工用「黏的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「倖存者」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「運輸」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「雜草」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**Answer: C**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：明亮的展場燈光干擾了野外工作站附近的動物。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**

## CHAPTER 19｜The Parade of Different Stars｜不同星星的遊行
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the river festival parade, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: a photographer posted pictures of visitors who had not agreed to be filmed. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在河畔節慶遊行，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：一名攝影師張貼了未同意入鏡的訪客照片。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "afterward", "approve", "audience", and "awaken". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A student tried afterward in a sentence about their journey. A listener asked what it meant to approve in an everyday choice. A listener remembered audience from an ordinary day. A volunteer used awaken in an example about responsibility. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著afterward（事後）、approve（核准）、audience（觀眾）、awaken（喚醒）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名學生在旅途句子中試著使用「事後」。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「核准」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「觀眾」。一名志工用「喚醒」舉出責任的例子。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "bull", "clown", "countable", and "deposit". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A neighbor talked about bull in a story about the town. A listener remembered clown from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really countable and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed deposit through a concrete detail, not a guess. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著bull（公牛）、clown（小丑）、countable（可逐項計算的）、deposit（押金）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一名鄰居把「公牛」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「小丑」。志工用「可逐項計算的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「押金」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: the event permit allowed a parade but said nothing about using faces in advertisements. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui requested the photographs be removed and offered a new photo area with clear choices. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當活動許可准許遊行，卻沒有同意把人臉用在廣告中，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安要求撤下照片，另設選擇清楚的拍照區。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "detect", "hollow", "original", and "parade". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. Someone asked when it would be right to detect. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really hollow and welcomed another view. A visitor said original did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about parade in a story about the town. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著detect（偵測）、hollow（中空的）、original（原始的）、parade（遊行）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。有人思考什麼時候該「偵測」，又該何時停下。志工用「中空的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。一名訪客說「原始的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「遊行」放入小鎮故事。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "performance", "photographer", "purse", and "reaction". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. One card about performance brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed photographer through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about purse in a story about the town. A listener remembered reaction from an ordinary day. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是performance（表演）、photographer（攝影師）、purse（錢包）、reaction（反應）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一張關於「表演」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「攝影師」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「錢包」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「反應」。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that some organizers said everyone at a public event should expect publicity. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: she stood by the consent signs even when the parade slowed down. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：部分主辦者說出席公開活動就應預期被宣傳。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：即使遊行因此放慢，她仍堅持同意告示。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "stitch", "truthful", "wheat", and "zone". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed stitch through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word truthful described their memory fairly. A listener remembered wheat from an ordinary day. One card about zone brought two different memories together. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：stitch（縫）、truthful（真實的）、wheat（小麥）、zone（區域）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「縫」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「真實的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「小麥」。一張關於「區域」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

### 本章新目標詞

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the river festival parade?**
A. A photographer posted pictures of visitors who had not agreed to be filmed.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：一名攝影師張貼了未同意入鏡的訪客照片。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Rui requested the photographs be removed and offered a new photo area with clear choices.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安要求撤下照片，另設選擇清楚的拍照區。

## CHAPTER 20｜Dawn at the Dam｜水壩邊的黎明
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached the valley dam visitor center, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that heavy weather cut the main road while families waited for a morning bus. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達山谷水壩遊客中心時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：惡劣天氣中斷主要道路，當時有家庭正等待早班公車。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "ambulance", "breath", "cattle", and "dam". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A neighbor talked about ambulance in a story about the town. A listener remembered breath from an ordinary day. One card about cattle brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed dam through a concrete detail, not a guess. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著ambulance（救護車）、breath（呼吸）、cattle（牛群）、dam（水壩）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一名鄰居把「救護車」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「呼吸」。一張關於「牛群」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「水壩」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "dawn", "dishonest", "disk", and "dislike". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A listener remembered dawn from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really dishonest and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed disk through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about dislike in a story about the town. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著dawn（黎明）、dishonest（不誠實的）、disk（磁碟）、dislike（不喜歡）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「黎明」。志工用「不誠實的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「磁碟」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「不喜歡」放入小鎮故事。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: a local emergency plan identified a safe room higher than the river. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui stopped the show and used its clear signs to guide people to shelter. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：當地緊急計畫標示出一間高於河面的安全室。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安停止演出，改用展覽清楚的標示引導人們避難。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "farther", "handy", "log", and "magnet". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. One note included farther and left the final question open. A visitor said handy did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about log in a story about the town. A listener remembered magnet from an ordinary day. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是farther（更遠的）、handy（方便的）、log（紀錄）、magnet（磁鐵）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一張便條寫上「更遠的」，卻保留最後的問題。一名訪客說「方便的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「紀錄」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「磁鐵」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "monk", "omit", "scatter", and "shrimp". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed monk through a concrete detail, not a guess. A listener asked what it meant to omit in an everyday choice. A listener remembered scatter from an ordinary day. One card about shrimp brought two different memories together. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：monk（僧侶）、omit（省略）、scatter（分散）、shrimp（蝦子）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「僧侶」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「省略」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「分散」。一張關於「蝦子」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
The sky show stopped, but the team still had a duty to help people follow the official shelter plan. After the last family had reached the safe room, Rui sat beside Jonas on a wooden step. He offered her tea, then asked whether she might like to see him away from work when the tour ended. She smiled, but said they should first finish the public review. He agreed without taking her delay as a rejection. They checked the updated notice together, making sure it contained no invented report about the dam. The quiet moment mattered to Rui because neither of them had to give up responsibility to be kind to the other.

**繁中**
星空展雖然停止，團隊仍有責任協助人們依照官方避難計畫行動。最後一戶家庭抵達安全室後，芮安坐在喬森身旁的木階上。他遞給她一杯茶，問她巡演結束後是否願意在工作之外與他見面。她微笑，但說應該先完成公開審查。他同意，沒有把她的延後當成拒絕。兩人一起核對更新公告，確保其中沒有捏造水壩狀況的報告。這個安靜時刻對芮安很重要，因為他們不必放棄責任，也能善待彼此。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "stable", "territory", "tough", and "verse". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A speaker asked whether the word stable described their memory fairly. A listener remembered territory from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really tough and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed verse through a concrete detail, not a guess. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著stable（穩固的）、territory（責任範圍）、tough（艱難的）、verse（詩節）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有人稱某段回憶是「穩固的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「責任範圍」。志工用「艱難的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「詩節」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：惡劣天氣中斷主要道路，當時有家庭正等待早班公車。
