## CHAPTER 21｜The Price of a Vote｜一張票的價值
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the public grant board while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until a sponsor offered to return if it could collect private visitor records. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在公共補助審查委員會時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：贊助商表示只要能收集觀眾私人資料就願意回來。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "bomb", "cheek", "crawl", and "dust". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. The bomb card became a discussion about how public alarms should be verified. One card about cheek brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed crawl through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about dust in a story about the town. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著bomb（炸彈）、cheek（臉頰）、crawl（爬行）、dust（灰塵）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。炸彈卡片引發了對公共警報應如何查證的討論。一張關於「臉頰」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「爬行」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「灰塵」放入小鎮故事。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "election", "hint", "marker", and "minus". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. One card about election brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed hint through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about marker in a story about the town. A listener remembered minus from an ordinary day. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是election（選舉）、hint（提示）、marker（記號筆）、minus（減去）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一張關於「選舉」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「提示」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「記號筆」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「減去」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: the old contract proved that free entry had never required personal tracking. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Rui showed the board a separate budget that did not depend on the sponsor. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：舊合約證明免費入場從未要求個人追蹤。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，芮安向委員會展示一份不依賴贊助商的獨立預算。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "moth", "oven", "pad", and "pollution". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed moth through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about oven in a story about the town. A listener remembered pad from an ordinary day. One card about pollution brought two different memories together. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：moth（飛蛾）、oven（烤箱）、pad（便條紙）、pollution（污染）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「飛蛾」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「烤箱」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「便條紙」。一張關於「污染」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "pure", "religious", "rid", and "scarce". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A speaker asked whether the word pure described their memory fairly. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly religious. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really rid and welcomed another view. A visitor said scarce did not mean the same thing to every family. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著pure（純淨的）、religious（宗教的）、rid（去除）、scarce（稀缺的）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有人稱某段回憶是「純淨的」，並說明原因。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「宗教的」。志工用「去除」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。一名訪客說「稀缺的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: some members warned that refusing money might reduce the tour. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, she accepted a smaller route rather than surrender audience data. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：部分委員警告拒絕資金可能縮減巡演規模。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：她接受較小的巡演路線，也不交出觀眾資料。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "spoil", "strategy", "thumb", and "wrap". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. Someone asked when it would be right to spoil. One card about strategy brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed thumb through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about wrap in a story about the town. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著spoil（弄壞）、strategy（策略）、thumb（拇指）、wrap（包裹）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。有人思考什麼時候該「弄壞」，又該何時停下。一張關於「策略」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「拇指」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「包裹」放入小鎮故事。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the public grant board?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
C. A sponsor offered to return if it could collect private visitor records.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：贊助商表示只要能收集觀眾私人資料就願意回來。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. Rui showed the board a separate budget that did not depend on the sponsor.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安向委員會展示一份不依賴贊助商的獨立預算。

## CHAPTER 22｜The Proof in the Voices｜聲音裡的證據
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the independent review workshop, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: the team had to prove its lessons worked without giving away participants' identities. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在獨立審查工作坊，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：團隊必須在不交出參與者身分的情況下證明課程有效。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "adventure", "beneath", "bind", and "cricket". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. One card about adventure brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed beneath through a concrete detail, not a guess. A listener asked what it meant to bind in an everyday choice. A listener remembered cricket from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是adventure（冒險）、beneath（在下面）、bind（綁住）、cricket（蟋蟀）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。一張關於「冒險」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「在下面」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「綁住」。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「蟋蟀」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "donkey", "fist", "hanger", and "kidney". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed donkey through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about fist in a story about the town. A listener remembered hanger from an ordinary day. One card about kidney brought two different memories together. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：donkey（驢子）、fist（拳頭）、hanger（衣架）、kidney（腎臟）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「驢子」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「拳頭」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「衣架」。一張關於「腎臟」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: anonymous feedback and observed repairs offered evidence of learning. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui shared methods and limits and let reviewers ask difficult questions. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當匿名回饋與實際修復紀錄提供了學習成果的證據，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安公開方法及限制，讓審查者提出困難問題。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "lord", "meanwhile", "oral", and "presence". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A neighbor talked about lord in a story about the town. A listener remembered meanwhile from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really oral and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed presence through a concrete detail, not a guess. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著lord（大人（戲稱））、meanwhile（同時）、oral（口頭的）、presence（存在）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名鄰居把「大人（戲稱）」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「同時」。志工用「口頭的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「存在」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "proof", "reasonable", "reserve", and "shallow". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered proof from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really reasonable and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed reserve through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word shallow described their memory fairly. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著proof（證據）、reasonable（合理的）、reserve（儲備）、shallow（淺的）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「證據」。志工用「合理的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「儲備」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「淺的」，並說明原因。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The difficult choice came after the practical work. Someone pointed out that a friendly adviser suggested exaggerating visitor numbers. Rui felt the familiar desire to keep everyone pleased; a traveling producer could lose a season of bookings after one angry message. Yet a pleasing answer was not always a fair one. She listened to the people affected and read the written agreement once more. Her response was clear: she rejected the suggestion and submitted the verified smaller total. Jonas did not make the choice for her. He asked whether she wanted his help carrying it out, and accepted her answer. Outside the dome, stars appeared slowly as the lights went down. Rui liked working beside him, but she liked even more that their work could remain open to criticism.

**繁中**
實際工作完成後，困難的選擇才出現。有人堅持：一位友善顧問建議誇大觀眾人數。芮安又想起自己總希望讓每個人滿意；巡迴策展人可能因一則憤怒訊息失去整季預約。然而討人喜歡的答案不一定公平。她傾聽受影響者的聲音，再讀一次書面協議，最後決定：她拒絕這項建議，提交經核對但較少的實際數字。喬森沒有替她決定，只問她是否希望自己協助執行，也尊重她的回答。星空帳篷外，燈光變暗，星星緩緩出現。芮安喜歡和他工作，但她更珍惜這份工作仍能接受批評。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "tablet", "tease", "toss", and "tricky". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about tablet brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to tease in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about toss in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly tricky. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著tablet（平板）、tease（取笑）、toss（拋擲）、tricky（棘手的）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「平板」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「取笑」。一名鄰居把「拋擲」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「棘手的」。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the independent review workshop?**
A. The team had to prove its lessons worked without giving away participants' identities.
B. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：團隊必須在不交出參與者身分的情況下證明課程有效。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
C. Rui shared methods and limits and let reviewers ask difficult questions.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安公開方法及限制，讓審查者提出困難問題。

## CHAPTER 23｜Back Where She Began｜回到起點的地方
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
By the time the volunteers reached Rui's hometown schoolyard, folding chairs were already waiting beneath the evening sky. Rui An, twenty-eight, led the tour because she believed that learning about stars could belong to ordinary people, not only to those who could buy tickets. Jonas Qiao, thirty-one, had designed the light that made their small canvas dome seem immense. They had argued about timing before, but never about the value of a clear exit. Then they learned that former classmates assumed she would move away if the project became successful. Rui asked the group not to hide the problem behind a smiling advertisement. She placed a blank sheet beside the entrance, ready for facts, questions, and corrections. A night's success would depend on what people could honestly decide together.

**繁中**
志工抵達芮安家鄉的學校操場時，折疊椅早已在暮色中排好。二十八歲的芮安帶領這場巡演，因為她相信星空知識應該屬於一般人，不該只有買得起門票的人才能接近。三十一歲的喬森設計了燈具，使小小的帆布帳篷看起來像無邊天空。他們曾為時程爭論，卻不會拿安全出口妥協。然而，大家得知：老同學認為專案一成功她就會搬走。芮安要求不要把問題藏在微笑的廣告後面。她在入口擺一張白紙，準備記下事實、問題和更正。一夜是否成功，取決於眾人能否誠實地共同決定。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "adviser", "bait", "bang", and "constant". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A volunteer discussed adviser through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about bait in a story about the town. A listener remembered bang from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really constant and welcomed another view. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：adviser（顧問）、bait（誘餌）、bang（砰聲）、constant（固定的）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。志工把「顧問」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「誘餌」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「砰聲」。志工用「固定的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "glory", "grassy", "heal", and "hesitate". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A neighbor talked about glory in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly grassy. A volunteer used heal in an example about responsibility. One speaker asked how to hesitate in a fair and careful way. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著glory（榮耀）、grassy（長草的）、heal（治癒）、hesitate（猶豫）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名鄰居把「榮耀」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「長草的」。一名志工用「治癒」舉出責任的例子。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「猶豫」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
The small team found a point they could actually test: a young apprentice had already learned to operate the small dome safely. Rui asked Jonas to describe the limit of the evidence before they discussed a solution. He did not pretend one measurement answered every question. Instead, they invited a local volunteer to observe the same test. Only after that did Rui invited local volunteers to plan next year's calendar without her approval. A few visitors were surprised to hear an organizer say that she did not know everything. Rui welcomed the doubt. If a child could challenge the explanation without being laughed at, then adults could challenge the budget and the safety plan too. They wrote the findings on the public board, even the parts that did not flatter the project.

**繁中**
小組找到一項真正可以檢驗的線索：一位年輕學徒已學會安全操作小型星空帳篷。芮安先請喬森說明證據的限制，再討論解法。他沒有假裝一次測量就能回答所有問題，而是邀請當地志工見證相同測試。之後，芮安邀請在地志工自行規劃明年的時程，不必經過她核准。有些訪客驚訝地聽到主辦者承認自己並非無所不知。芮安歡迎質疑。若孩子可以提出問題而不被嘲笑，大人也應能質疑預算與安全計畫。他們把結果寫上公開板，連那些不利於專案形象的部分也保留下來。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "hometown", "hum", "import", and "jeep". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered hometown from an ordinary day. A volunteer used hum in an example about responsibility. A volunteer discussed import through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about jeep in a story about the town. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著hometown（故鄉）、hum（隱約發出嗡聲）、import（進口）、jeep（吉普車）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「故鄉」。一名志工用「隱約發出嗡聲」舉出責任的例子。志工把「進口」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「吉普車」放入小鎮故事。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "koala", "napkin", "onto", and "pub". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about koala brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed napkin through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about onto in a story about the town. A listener remembered pub from an ordinary day. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著koala（無尾熊）、napkin（餐巾）、onto（到……上）、pub（酒館）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「無尾熊」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「餐巾」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「到……上」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「酒館」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
The schoolyard felt smaller than Rui remembered, but the students made the new dome seem large enough for everyone. When the last chair was folded, Jonas told her about the teaching offer. He wanted to take it, even though he also wanted to keep traveling with her when he could. Rui was relieved; she had been accepted into a course on public exhibition design and did not want to abandon it. They decided to try a relationship in which two careers could grow. Jonas asked if a slow walk after the closing fair could count as their first date. Rui said yes. It was a choice made in private, without giving either of them control over the other's work.

**繁中**
家鄉的操場比芮安記憶中小，但學生讓新的星空帳篷看起來足以容納所有人。收好最後一張椅子時，喬森告訴她外地教學工作的邀約。他想接受，也希望有空時繼續陪她巡演。芮安鬆了口氣，因為她已獲公共展覽設計課程錄取，也不想放棄。兩人決定嘗試一段能讓雙方事業一起成長的關係。喬森問，閉幕市集之後的慢慢散步能否算是第一次約會。芮安答應了。這是他們私下的決定，並沒有讓任何一方取得控制對方工作的權力。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "ray", "scary", "sensible", and "trace". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed ray through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word scary described their memory fairly. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly sensible. One card about trace brought two different memories together. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是ray（光線）、scary（可怕的）、sensible（明智的）、trace（痕跡）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「光線」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「可怕的」，並說明原因。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「明智的」。一張關於「痕跡」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**1. What was the main problem at Rui's hometown schoolyard?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. Former classmates assumed she would move away if the project became successful.
C. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：老同學認為專案一成功她就會搬走。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. Rui invited local volunteers to plan next year's calendar without her approval.
B. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: A**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安邀請在地志工自行規劃明年的時程，不必經過她核准。

## CHAPTER 24｜The Last Breakdown｜最後一次故障
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
The van stopped at the road to the closing fair while the sky still held a line of daylight. Rui unfolded the community schedule, and Jonas counted tools before he carried the dome into the gathering space. Their project combined simple astronomy with stories written by visitors. People could speak, stay silent, or remove a recording later. The rule seemed easy until their transport vehicle broke down after the sponsor formally withdrew. Some helpers suggested moving ahead and explaining afterward. Rui would not. She had learned that a promise made to a crowd was easy to celebrate and difficult to repair once broken. She began with the question nobody wanted to put on the poster: who would carry the cost if the team made the wrong choice?

**繁中**
貨車停在前往閉幕市集的道路時，天空還留著一道日光。芮安攤開社區行程，喬森清點工具後才將星空帳篷搬入集會空間。他們的計畫把簡單天文知識與訪客寫下的故事結合。人們可以開口、保持沉默，或日後撤回錄音。這項原則原本很容易，直到發生了這件事：贊助商正式退出後，他們的運輸車輛又故障了。幾名助手建議先辦再說明，芮安卻不同意。她明白對眾人許下承諾容易，失信後要修復卻很難。她先問出沒有人想印在海報上的問題：如果團隊選錯，究竟由誰承擔代價？

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "airline", "arrest", "attitude", and "cheerful". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. A neighbor talked about airline in a story about the town. A listener remembered arrest from an ordinary day. One card about attitude brought two different memories together. A visitor said cheerful did not mean the same thing to every family. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著airline（航空公司）、arrest（逮捕）、attitude（態度）、cheerful（開朗的）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。一名鄰居把「航空公司」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「逮捕」。一張關於「態度」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。一名訪客說「開朗的」對每個家庭不一定相同。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "chilly", "dare", "dock", and "harmful". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly chilly. A volunteer used dare in an example about responsibility. A volunteer discussed dock through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word harmful described their memory fairly. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著chilly（寒冷的）、dare（敢於）、dock（碼頭）、harmful（有害的）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「寒冷的」。一名志工用「敢於」舉出責任的例子。志工把「碼頭」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「有害的」，並說明原因。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Something changed when they checked a detail nobody had thought important: the engine log showed a known problem that had not been repaired. Jonas looked at Rui and said that careful work often began with admitting an ordinary mistake. She wanted to protect the volunteers from blame, but protection did not mean pretending the problem was harmless. Together they reviewed the safest available options. Then Jonas called a licensed repair service while Rui arranged a smaller local program. This time they described not only what they would do, but what might still go wrong. A visitor wrote down the remaining question. Rui left it visible throughout the event, because an honest gap was better than a confident answer that could not survive a simple test.

**繁中**
他們檢查一個原本不被重視的細節後，事情有了轉變：引擎紀錄顯示一項早已知悉卻尚未修復的問題。喬森看著芮安說，仔細工作往往從承認普通的失誤開始。她想保護志工不受責怪，卻也知道保護不等於假裝問題沒有危險。兩人一同檢視最安全的方案，接著，喬森聯繫合格維修單位，芮安安排規模較小的在地節目。這回他們不只說明將做什麼，也說明還可能發生什麼。一名訪客寫下未解的問題。芮安讓它整晚留在顯眼處，因為誠實承認未知，比經不起測試的自信答案更好。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "helmet", "jungle", "loser", and "navy". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. One card about helmet brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed jungle through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about loser in a story about the town. A listener remembered navy from an ordinary day. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著helmet（安全帽）、jungle（叢林（比喻錯綜複雜））、loser（輸家）、navy（海軍）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。一張關於「安全帽」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「叢林（比喻錯綜複雜）」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「輸家」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「海軍」。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "promote", "rag", "sigh", and "suffer". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. One speaker asked how to promote in a fair and careful way. A neighbor talked about rag in a story about the town. A listener remembered sigh from an ordinary day. A volunteer used suffer in an example about responsibility. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是promote（推廣）、rag（破布）、sigh（嘆氣）、suffer（承受）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「推廣」。一名鄰居把「破布」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「嘆氣」。一名志工用「承受」舉出責任的例子。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
There was a moment when the whole tour seemed to hang on one reply: the closing venue could not wait for all the equipment to arrive. Rui looked at the visitors who had trusted her with time, stories, and silence. She could not ask them to carry a cost they had never agreed to. After hearing their views, they refused an unsafe shortcut and carried only tested portable materials. She spoke plainly, without turning the refusal into a speech about her own courage. Jonas stood nearby, ready to explain the technical side, but he did not interrupt her. On the way back to the van, they talked about food, sleep, and how much distance each of them still needed. Their closeness grew through small freedoms, not public declarations.

**繁中**
有一瞬間，整場巡演彷彿繫於一個回答：閉幕場地無法等所有設備抵達才開始。芮安望著把時間、故事與沉默交給她的觀眾，知道不能要他們承擔從未同意的代價。聽過大家的意見後，她決定：兩人拒絕不安全的捷徑，只帶上經測試的輕便材料。她直白說明，不把拒絕化成歌頌自己勇氣的演說。喬森站在附近，準備解釋技術問題，卻沒有打斷她。返回貨車途中，兩人聊食物、睡眠，以及彼此仍需要多少個人空間。親近來自細小的自由，不是公開宣言。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "talent", "vacant", "vehicle", and "warn". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A neighbor talked about talent in a story about the town. Someone wondered whether the situation was truly vacant. One card about vehicle brought two different memories together. One speaker asked how to warn in a fair and careful way. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：talent（天賦）、vacant（空蕩的）、vehicle（車輛）、warn（警告）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名鄰居把「天賦」放入小鎮故事。有人思考當時的情況是否真的「空蕩的」。一張關於「車輛」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。有位講述者說人應該學著自己「警告」。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

### 理解測驗
**1. What was the main problem at the road to the closing fair?**
A. Everyone had agreed that the program should be canceled forever.
B. The star dome had already been sold to a new owner.
C. Their transport vehicle broke down after the sponsor formally withdrew.
**Answer: C**　繁中解析：本章的實際問題是：贊助商正式退出後，他們的運輸車輛又故障了。

**2. How did Rui and the team respond to the situation?**
A. They kept the problem secret and continued without changes.
B. Jonas called a licensed repair service while Rui arranged a smaller local program.
C. They asked visitors to pay for a false explanation.
**Answer: B**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：喬森聯繫合格維修單位，芮安安排規模較小的在地節目。

## CHAPTER 25｜A Sky That Stays｜會留下來的星空
### 正文與翻譯
**EN**
At the harvest community fair, Rui An arrived before sunset with a portable star dome, a box of paper story cards, and two open doors to keep clear. At twenty-eight, she had spent a year saving for this traveling exhibition. Jonas Qiao, the thirty-one-year-old engineer who built its turning lamp, checked each support instead of praising the view. Tonight's immediate difficulty was clear: residents asked who would own the project after the final performance. Rui wrote the problem where every helper could see it. She separated the facts they had checked from the claims they had merely heard. This was meant to be a free show, not a secret test of visitors. 

**繁中**
在豐收社區市集，芮安於日落前帶著移動式星空帳篷、一盒紙製故事卡和必須保持暢通的兩個出入口抵達。二十八歲的她花了一年為這場巡迴展覽存錢。三十一歲、設計旋轉燈具的機械師喬森逐一檢查支架，而不是忙著讚美風景。今晚的困難很清楚：居民詢問最後一場演出後，專案究竟會屬於誰。芮安把問題寫在所有志工看得見的地方，將已查證的事實與只聽說的傳言分開。這應該是免費展覽，而不是觀眾不知情的測試。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read "agriculture", "cart", "cleaner", and "crop". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. A listener remembered agriculture from an ordinary day. One card about cart brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed cleaner through a concrete detail, not a guess. A neighbor talked about crop in a story about the town. An older neighbor explained one card through a small object that could be passed from hand to hand. A younger person offered a different scene, and the two were allowed to disagree.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著agriculture（農業）、cart（手推車）、cleaner（清潔人員）、crop（作物）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「農業」。一張關於「手推車」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「清潔人員」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名鄰居把「作物」放入小鎮故事。一位年長鄰居用能在手中傳遞的小物件解釋其中一張卡，年輕人則提出不同的場景；兩人被允許保留分歧。

**EN**
At the second story circle, the volunteers turned over four cards marked "educational", "efficient", "gossip", and "heap". Nobody was asked for a perfect definition or a personal secret. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really educational and welcomed another view. A visitor said efficient did not mean the same thing to every family. A neighbor talked about gossip in a story about the town. A listener remembered heap from an ordinary day. One speaker described a scene from work; another remembered a journey, while a third chose to leave the card unanswered. Rui crossed out no one's silence.

**繁中**
在第二輪故事圈，志工翻出四張卡，分別寫著educational（教育的）、efficient（有效率的）、gossip（閒話）、heap（一堆）。沒有人被要求說出完美的定義或私人祕密。志工用「教育的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。一名訪客說「有效率的」對每個家庭不一定相同。一名鄰居把「閒話」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「一堆」。有人說了工作中的場景，有人想起一段旅程，第三個人則選擇不回答。芮安沒有抹去任何人的沉默。

**EN**
They did not have to guess about everything. An important clue emerged: the new charter gave each town a voice in the equipment and story archive. Jonas repeated the observation with another volunteer watching, then wrote down both the result and the conditions. Rui was tempted to solve the matter herself, but a traveling show could become a private kingdom if every choice belonged to one person. So Rui handed over the shared manual and helped a new team lead the final star lesson. Their plan left room for someone to disagree. They checked the path, explained the change aloud, and kept a written record that the next town could examine. 

**繁中**
並不是每件事都得靠猜測。當新的章程讓每個小鎮都能參與設備及故事檔案的決定，他們找到重要線索。喬森在另一名志工見證下重做觀察，將結果與條件一起記下。芮安原本想自己解決，卻想到如果所有選擇都由她一人決定，巡迴展也會變成私人王國。因此，芮安交出共享手冊，協助新團隊主導最後一堂星空課。他們的計畫容許別人反對。他們檢查路線、公開解釋變動，留下可供下一個小鎮查閱的文字紀錄。

**EN**
The next set of four prompts was "heel", "innocent", "lobby", and "location". Jonas moved the lamp a little lower so that the cards could be read without forcing people to stand. A volunteer discussed heel through a concrete detail, not a guess. A speaker asked whether the word innocent described their memory fairly. A listener remembered lobby from an ordinary day. One card about location brought two different memories together. The words did not explain the sky by themselves. They became useful only when a real person placed them next to something seen, heard, or tested.

**繁中**
下一組四個提示是heel（腳跟）、innocent（無辜的）、lobby（大廳）、location（位置）。喬森把燈調低一些，讓大家不必站起來也能讀清楚卡片。志工把「腳跟」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。有人稱某段回憶是「無辜的」，並說明原因。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「大廳」。一張關於「位置」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。那些字本身無法解釋天空。只有真人把它們放在親眼看過、親耳聽見或實際查證的事情旁邊，它們才有用。

**EN**
Four more words arrived from the visitors' notebooks: "neighborhood", "plenty", "raw", and "rectangle". Rui listened for what the speakers wanted to keep, rather than for a line that would sound clever in an advertisement. A neighbor talked about neighborhood in a story about the town. A listener remembered plenty from an ordinary day. A volunteer wondered if a scene was really raw and welcomed another view. A volunteer discussed rectangle through a concrete detail, not a guess. The talk moved from easy memories to questions about who was allowed to speak and who would carry the results home. Rui reminded the group that permission could change tomorrow.

**繁中**
訪客筆記裡又出現四個詞：neighborhood（社區）、plenty（充足的）、raw（未加工的）、rectangle（長方形）。芮安在意的是講述者想保留什麼，而不是能否摘出適合廣告的漂亮台詞。一名鄰居把「社區」放入小鎮故事。一位聽眾想起日常生活裡的「充足的」。志工用「未加工的」形容一個場景，也歡迎別的看法。志工把「長方形」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。對話從輕鬆回憶轉向誰有權發言，以及結果由誰帶回家。芮安提醒大家，同意可以在明天改變。

**EN**
The last evening was not a farewell to the stars. At the harvest fair, Rui saw two young volunteers lead the dome without looking to her for permission. The new agreement let each town choose its own stories, protect its recordings, and lend equipment to another town. After the public vote, Jonas found her beside the folding door. He would teach mechanics two days a week and join the tour whenever their schedules allowed. Rui would begin her design course in spring. 'Are we still going on that first date?' she asked. 'I hoped you would remember,' he answered. Their kiss was brief and private. Then they returned to the square to help the new team pack the dome, leaving its next story in other hands.

**繁中**
最後一晚不是向星星告別。在豐收市集，芮安看見兩名年輕志工自行帶領展覽，已不必抬頭向她請示。新協議讓每個小鎮自行選擇故事、保護錄音，也能把設備借給下一站。公開投票結束後，喬森在折疊門旁找到她。他每週會有兩天教授機械，行程允許時再加入巡演；芮安則會在春天開始設計課程。「我們還要去第一次約會嗎？」她問。「我還希望妳記得呢。」他回答。兩人短暫而私密地接吻，然後回到廣場，幫新的團隊收起帳篷，把下一段故事交到其他人手裡。

**EN**
The last cards of this round were "shrink", "slice", "suck", and "tend". Rui kept the circle open even when the words did not seem to belong to the same story. Someone asked when it would be right to shrink. One card about slice brought two different memories together. A volunteer discussed suck through a concrete detail, not a guess. A listener asked what it meant to tend in an everyday choice. Some visitors wanted to turn the words into a single cheerful story. Others found the same cards difficult.

**繁中**
這一輪最後幾張卡寫著shrink（縮小）、slice（薄片）、suck（吸取）、tend（照顧）。即使這些字似乎不屬於同一個故事，芮安仍讓故事圈保持開放。有人思考什麼時候該「縮小」，又該何時停下。一張關於「薄片」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。志工把「吸取」和具體細節連結，而不是靠猜測。一名聽眾問，在日常選擇裡該如何理解「照顧」。有些訪客想把這些詞寫成單一的快樂故事，其他人卻覺得同樣的卡片很難面對。

**EN**
Rui opened four prompts from the community word box. The cards read , and "tunnel". She asked why a single word could make two people remember different nights. One card about tunnel brought two different memories together.

**繁中**
芮安從社區詞語盒打開四張提示卡，寫著tunnel（隧道）。她問大家，為什麼同一個字會讓兩個人想起不同的夜晚。一張關於「隧道」的卡片連起兩段不同回憶。

**Answer: C**　繁中解析：團隊採取的行動是：芮安交出共享手冊，協助新團隊主導最後一堂星空課。
